# Exact V304 installation and the previously unexecuted verification tail pass

The actual public installer is exercised in an isolated directory with file:// release fixtures. It installs the preserved prior release, upgrades to exact V304, passes all 31 installed end-to-end checks, restores the exact prior activation and executes a real rollback generation that returns OK. All five phases pass in 86.458171333 seconds under protocol 873a7f5d8d659bc5f3e85b6a08260ec998e1551ad3be0e0fcfffcc82313849e7. Both installed binary and Metal bytes are checked against the frozen artifacts. The user's installation and shell configuration are untouched. This is real installation/compatibility evidence, not default activation or publication.

The full remaining tail's original 15-minute-plus-cleanup admission no longer fits after installation; that attempt creates no successor directory or child. The existing source is split at its vision section into independently bounded subsets. No request, image, output length, context, capacity, check expression or acceptance threshold is shortened. Both subsets preserve the same original header, selected binary, cleanup trap, strict skip/failure handling and source/driver identity checks. The original full-verification resource failures and earlier headroom refusal remain preserved.

V315 runs the original text-quality, symlink and API source section under a 600-second outer ceiling. All 15 behavioural probes, real symlinked model loading and all 74 API robustness checks pass; the subset summary is three passed and zero failed. It completes in 226.113018959 seconds under protocol 085ac0e51a6e66ad024ca6f6cc353a9742a2588e2297c61174b8c8216d7341e5.

V316 runs the exact original vision section under a 420-second outer ceiling. Native pixels/embeddings are compared with the independent mlx0.31.1 float32 reference; both tower checks pass. Original photographs, 14.5 GB serving target, 3072 prefill chunk, 32768 context and original known-content/serving error cases are unchanged. All 25 serving checks pass; the subset summary is three passed and zero failed. It completes in 125.058958625 seconds under protocol 6eeacb65aad532cea8d1bea3300aa1bace5ceba440f7e3aba8ddea64c460c3c4.

Every previously unexecuted original tail assertion is now covered on V304. The earlier elastic, MTP, long and 2K resource exclusions are still unresolved; tail success cannot claim a full no-VM verification pass. These later runs are correctness/compatibility work, not performance measurements. A few bounded source/evidence reads occur after prose timing has ended. All selected source/binary/Metal and script identities remain unchanged during their runs, workers drain, and no skipped gate is credited.

Root hands back at 17:54:55 UTC, before the hard 18:00 deadline, with the model lock free and no remaining model/compiler/server process. The peer owns subsequent Mac work. A future 110-minute interval is requested but not reserved. Source/evidence and small model-free readiness work continue; no app is closed and no default is activated.

````text
== install integrity ==
PASS  installed binary reports a version
PASS  metallib shipped beside the binary
PASS  doctor runs with no model loaded
PASS  doctor refuses unavailable memory on a busy 16 GB Mac

== weights-free gates from the installed binary ==
PASS  sampler golden (greedy)
PASS  sampler golden (nucleus + penalty)
PASS  governor policy branches
PASS  chat template matches transformers
== API surface ==
PASS  /api/version matches the binary (0.2.11)
PASS  /api/tags lists the model
PASS  /api/ps reports a loaded model
PASS  /api/show carries the memory plan
PASS  /api/show carries prefix cache stats
PASS  /v1/models (OpenAI surface)
PASS  HEAD returns no body
PASS  malformed JSON gets 400
== generation: short, long, unicode, formats ==
PASS  short prompt, non-streamed
PASS  long prompt (~3.4k tokens)
PASS  unicode round-trip (emoji + CJK)
PASS  streamed deltas reassemble to non-streamed text
== OpenAI surface ==
PASS  OpenAI array-form content
== sampling knobs and hostile inputs ==
PASS  seed -1 (Ollama default) survives
PASS  num_predict -1 generates
PASS  top_p 0 clamped, not divide-by-zero
PASS  no-messages chat is the load request
PASS  over-length prompt refused with a typed 400
PASS  stop sequence honored (got '1 2 3 ')
== conversation prefix cache, live ==
PASS  follow-up turn reused a cached prefix (0 -> 1 hits)
== concurrency and liveness ==
PASS  4 concurrent clients all returned valid generations
PASS  server survives a client vanishing mid-stream

e2e: passed 31, failed 0
````

````text
from pathlib import Path
import datetime,json,os,re,signal,sys,time
ROOT=Path('/Users/carlos/Projects/slotstream');P=Path(__file__).parent
sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'Tools'))
from prefill_bench import digest,preflight,vm_snapshot
from serve_bench import competing_jobs,verified_build
from optimization_readiness import pressure_snapshot,require_normal
from optimization_serial_build import guarded_run
import optimization_campaign as campaign
B=ROOT/'.build/optimization/joined-state-eval-build-v304/candidate/slotstream'
OUT=ROOT/'.build/optimization/current-candidate-gates-v307/installed'
# The reservation must be granted by the peer before invocation; the argument
# binds a concrete handback deadline and never authorizes extending it.
DEADLINE=datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(sys.argv[1].replace('Z','+00:00')).timestamp()
remaining=int(DEADLINE-time.time()-30)
if remaining<900:raise RuntimeError('installed E2E plus actual rollback requires at least fifteen reserved minutes')
POLICY={'startup_reclaimable_bytes':13_000_000_000,'minimum_live_reclaimable_bytes':3_000_000_000,
 'maximum_owned_rss_bytes':13_000_000_000,'sample_interval_seconds':.2,
 'maximum_build_seconds':min(3600,remaining),'stop_on_new_swapouts':False}
NATIVE_PACKET=ROOT/'.build/optimization/joined-state-eval-campaign-v305'
native=campaign.status(NATIVE_PACKET)['stages'][:3]
assert [r['stage'] for r in native]==['native/'+n for n in campaign.NATIVE] and all(r['status']=='passed' for r in native)
PRIOR=Path('/tmp/slotstream-context-implementation-20260906/rollback-v0.2.11')
OLD=json.loads((PRIOR/'manifest.json').read_text());PRIOR_FILES={x['file']:x['sha256'] for x in OLD['files']}
assert all(digest(PRIOR/n)==h for n,h in PRIOR_FILES.items())
S={'prior_files':PRIOR_FILES,'prior_manifest_sha256':digest(PRIOR/'manifest.json'),'model':'/Users/carlos/.slotstream/models/qwen38-flash-next-mlx-4bit','classification':'Actual public install.sh under its explicit isolated root and file:// release fixture. Installs preserved public release, upgrades to exact V304, runs complete installed E2E, restores original release with same installer and proves actual rollback generation. No user install, profile, wrapper, published release, app closure, compiler or paid service.',
 'frozen_at':datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),'deadline_utc':sys.argv[1],
 'build':verified_build(B),'native_prerequisites':native,'native_contract_sha256':digest(NATIVE_PACKET/'qualification-contract.json'),'wrapper_sha256':digest(Path(__file__)),'policy':POLICY,
 'environment':{'SLOTSTREAM_TEST_BINARY':str(B),'SLOTSTREAM_VERIFY_OUT':str(OUT/'verification')},
 'command':['python3',str(P/'worker.py')],
 'worker_sha256':digest(P/'worker.py'),'installer_sha256':digest(ROOT/'install.sh'),
 'source_verification_sha256':digest(ROOT/'Tools/verify.sh'),
 'historical_counterexample':str(ROOT/'.build/optimization/full-verification-v283/manifest.json'),
 'drivers':{str(f.relative_to(ROOT)):digest(f) for f in sorted((ROOT/'Tools').rglob('*')) if f.is_file() and f.suffix in ['.sh','.py','.mjs','.json','.swift','.txt'] and '__pycache__' not in f.parts},
 'limits':'No build, app closure, default activation or paid service. Existing verified local model only. Source/binary/Metal pinned; files saved before exit.'}
with (P/'protocol.json').open('x') as f:f.write(json.dumps(S,indent=2)+'\n')
OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=False);started=time.monotonic();r={'passed':False,'protocol_sha256':digest(P/'protocol.json')}
def interrupted(number,_frame):raise KeyboardInterrupt(f'full qualification interrupted by signal{number}')
signal.signal(signal.SIGINT,interrupted);signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM,interrupted)
saved={k:v for k,v in os.environ.items() if k.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_','SS_DEBUG'))}
with (OUT/'pressure.samples.jsonl').open('x') as observations:
 def snapshot():
  state=vm_snapshot();pressure=pressure_snapshot();observations.write(json.dumps({'seconds':time.monotonic()-started,'vm':state,'pressure':pressure})+'\n');observations.flush();require_normal(pressure);return state
 try:
  r['before']=preflight(13);snapshot();assert not competing_jobs()
  assert (ROOT/'.venv31/bin/python').exists()
  for k in saved:os.environ.pop(k,None)
  os.environ.update(S['environment'])
  with (OUT/'stdout.txt').open('w') as stdout,(OUT/'stderr.txt').open('w') as stderr:
   result=guarded_run(S['command'],cwd=ROOT,stdout=stdout,stderr=stderr,record_path=OUT/'memory.json',snapshot=snapshot,policy=POLICY,classification=S['classification'])
  r['exit_code']=result.returncode
  report=json.loads((OUT/'delivery/manifest.json').read_text())
  r['delivery_report']=report
  r['passed']=result.returncode==0 and report.get('passed') is True and report.get('prior_activation')==report.get('restored_activation') and report.get('candidate_activation')!=report.get('prior_activation') and [x['name'] for x in report['rows']]==['prior-install','candidate-upgrade','installed-e2e','prior-rollback','rollback-generation'] and all(x['passed'] is True for x in report['rows'])
 except BaseException as e:r['error']=f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 finally:
  for k in list(os.environ):
   if k.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_','SS_DEBUG')):os.environ.pop(k,None)
  os.environ.update(saved)
  r['elapsed_seconds']=time.monotonic()-started
  # Primary result is durable before independent restoration checks.
  (OUT/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2)+'\n')
  for n,f in {'after':vm_snapshot,'pressure':pressure_snapshot,'candidate_unchanged':lambda:verified_build(B)==S['build'],
   'drivers_unchanged':lambda:all(digest(ROOT/n)==h for n,h in S['drivers'].items()) and digest(P/'worker.py')==S['worker_sha256'] and all(digest(PRIOR/n)==h for n,h in PRIOR_FILES.items()) and digest(ROOT/'install.sh')==S['installer_sha256'],
   'remaining_jobs':competing_jobs,'model_lock_free':lambda:bool(preflight(0))}.items():
   try:r[n]=f()
   except BaseException as e:r.setdefault('cleanup_errors',{})[n]=f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
  r['within_reservation']=time.time()<=DEADLINE
  r['passed']=bool(r['passed'] and not r.get('cleanup_errors') and r.get('candidate_unchanged') and r.get('drivers_unchanged')
   and not r.get('remaining_jobs') and r.get('model_lock_free') and r.get('pressure',{}).get('level')==1 and r['within_reservation'])
  (OUT/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2)+'\n')
print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in r.items() if k not in ['before','after']}),flush=True)
raise SystemExit(0 if r['passed'] else 1)
````

## /tmp/slotstream-optimization-execution/current-candidate-gates-v307/installed/worker.py

SHA-256 `dd75cf530858d2d1d04f73770896456c3c8ae9b3bbeb3f448b13baea9c7cd9c5`.

````text
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib,json,os,socket,subprocess,sys,tarfile,time
ROOT=Path('/Users/carlos/Projects/slotstream');P=Path(__file__).parent;sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'Tools'))
from prefill_bench import digest,preflight,run_child,terminate_child_tree,vm_snapshot
from serve_bench import wait_ready
S=json.loads((P/'protocol.json').read_text());OUT=ROOT/'.build/optimization/current-candidate-gates-v307/installed';BASE=OUT/'delivery';BASE.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
INSTALL=BASE/'installation';PRIOR=Path('/tmp/slotstream-context-implementation-20260906/rollback-v0.2.11');CANDIDATE=Path(S['build']['binary']).parent
assert not INSTALL.exists() and not INSTALL.is_symlink()
ENV={k:v for k,v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_','SS_DEBUG')) and k not in ('BIN','OPENAI_API_KEY','ANTHROPIC_API_KEY','AI_GATEWAY_API_KEY','VERCEL_OIDC_TOKEN')}
ENV.update(NO_PROXY='*')
r={'passed':False,'scope':'Isolated actual public installer upgrade, installed candidate E2E, and prior-release rollback. User installation and shell configuration are untouched.','rows':[]}
start=time.monotonic()
def persist():(BASE/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2)+'\n')
def step(name,command,limit=180,environment=None,validate=None):
 cell=BASE/name;cell.mkdir();row={'name':name,'command':command,'passed':False};r['rows'].append(row)
 try:
  row['exit_code']=run_child(command,{**ENV,**(environment or {})},cell,limit)
  if row['exit_code']!=0:raise RuntimeError(f'{name} exited {row["exit_code"]}')
  if validate:validate(cell)
  row['passed']=True
 finally:
  for n in ['stdout.txt','stderr.txt']:
   if (cell/n).exists():row[n+'_sha256']=digest(cell/n)
  (cell/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(row,indent=2)+'\n');persist()
 return cell

def assets(name,source,expected):
 out=BASE/'assets'/name;out.mkdir(parents=True)
 for n,h in expected.items():assert digest(source/n)==h,(name,n)
 archive=out/'slotstream-arm64.tar.gz'
 with tarfile.open(archive,'w:gz') as tar:
  for n in ['slotstream','mlx.metallib']:tar.add(source/n,arcname=n,recursive=False)
 (out/'slotstream-arm64.tar.gz.sha256').write_text(digest(archive)+'  slotstream-arm64.tar.gz\n')
 return out

def verify_install(expected):
 assert (INSTALL/'bin').is_symlink()
 for n,h in expected.items():assert digest(INSTALL/'bin'/n)==h,('installed bytes',n)
 return os.readlink(INSTALL/'bin')

def install(name,fixture,expected):
 env={'PATH':str(INSTALL/'bin')+os.pathsep+ENV['PATH'],'SLOTSTREAM_ROOT_DIR':str(INSTALL),'SLOTSTREAM_RELEASE_BASE':fixture.as_uri(),'SLOTSTREAM_MACOS_MAJOR':'26'}
 cell=step(name,['sh',str(ROOT/'install.sh')],180,env)
 assert 'installed slotstream ' in (cell/'stdout.txt').read_text()
 assert 'next:' in (cell/'stdout.txt').read_text(), 'installer must not start an unbounded interactive server'
 return verify_install(expected)
try:
 old=S['prior_files'];new={'slotstream':S['build']['identity']['binary_sha256'],'mlx.metallib':S['build']['identity']['metallib_sha256']}
 prior_assets=assets('prior',PRIOR,old);candidate_assets=assets('candidate',CANDIDATE,new)
 first=install('prior-install',prior_assets,old);r['prior_activation']=first
 current=install('candidate-upgrade',candidate_assets,new);r['candidate_activation']=current;assert first!=current
 port=11640
 with socket.socket() as available:available.bind(('127.0.0.1',port))
 preflight(13);server_dir=BASE/'installed-server';server_dir.mkdir()
 command=[str(INSTALL/'bin/slotstream'),'serve','--model',S['model'],'--port',str(port),'--memory-gb','10','--mtp','off','--vision','off','--max-context','32768','--max-prefill-wait','0','--no-elastic']
 r['server']={'command':command,'installed_binary_sha256':digest(INSTALL/'bin/slotstream'),'passed':False}
 with (server_dir/'stdout.txt').open('w') as out,(server_dir/'stderr.txt').open('w') as err:
  server=subprocess.Popen(command,cwd=ROOT,env=ENV,stdout=out,stderr=err,start_new_session=True)
  try:
   wait_ready(server,port)
   def check_e2e(cell):
    import re
    text=(cell/'stdout.txt').read_text();summary=re.findall(r'^e2e: passed (\d+), failed (\d+)$',text,re.M)
    assert len(summary)==1 and int(summary[0][0])>0 and summary[0][1]=='0'
    assert not re.search(r'^(FAIL|SKIP)\b',text,re.M)
    r['e2e_summary']=summary
   step('installed-e2e',['bash',str(ROOT/'Tools/e2e_release.sh'),str(port)],1800,{'BIN':str(INSTALL/'bin/slotstream')},check_e2e)
   r['server']['passed']=True
  finally:
   if server.poll() is None:terminate_child_tree(server)
   r['server']['exit_code']=server.returncode;persist()
 rollback=install('prior-rollback',prior_assets,old);assert rollback==first
 r['restored_activation']=rollback
 preflight(13)
 def check_rollback(cell):assert (cell/'stdout.txt').read_text().strip()=='OK'
 step('rollback-generation',[str(INSTALL/'bin/slotstream'),'run','--model',S['model'],'--memory-gb','10','--mtp','off','--vision','off','--greedy','--max-tokens','8','--prompt','Reply with exactly: OK'],180,validate=check_rollback)
 verify_install(old);r['passed']=True
except BaseException as e:r['error']=f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
finally:r['elapsed_seconds']=time.monotonic()-start;persist()
print(json.dumps({'passed':r['passed'],'rows':len(r['rows']),'e2e':r.get('e2e_summary'),'error':r.get('error')}),flush=True)
raise SystemExit(0 if r['passed'] else 1)

````

````text
from pathlib import Path
import datetime,json,os,re,shutil,signal,sys,time
ROOT=Path('/Users/carlos/Projects/slotstream');P=Path(__file__).parent
sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'Tools'))
from prefill_bench import digest,preflight,vm_snapshot
from serve_bench import competing_jobs,verified_build
from optimization_readiness import pressure_snapshot,require_normal
from optimization_serial_build import guarded_run
import optimization_campaign as campaign
B=ROOT/'.build/optimization/joined-state-eval-build-v304/candidate/slotstream'
OUT=ROOT/'.build/optimization/current-candidate-gates-v315/text-tail'
# The reservation must be granted by the peer before invocation; the argument
# binds a concrete handback deadline and never authorizes extending it.
DEADLINE=datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(sys.argv[1].replace('Z','+00:00')).timestamp()
remaining=int(DEADLINE-time.time()-30)
if remaining<600:raise RuntimeError('original text-quality/symlink/API subset requires at least10minutes within the granted interval')
POLICY={'startup_reclaimable_bytes':21_000_000_000,'minimum_live_reclaimable_bytes':3_000_000_000,
 'maximum_owned_rss_bytes':17_500_000_000,'sample_interval_seconds':.2,
 'maximum_build_seconds':min(600,remaining),'stop_on_new_swapouts':False}
NATIVE_PACKET=ROOT/'.build/optimization/joined-state-eval-campaign-v305'
native=campaign.status(NATIVE_PACKET)['stages'][:3]
assert [r['stage'] for r in native]==['native/'+n for n in campaign.NATIVE] and all(r['status']=='passed' for r in native)
S={'classification':'Only the unchanged, previously unexecuted TEXT subset of the full-verification tail: behavioural quality15, symlink model, and API robustness74. Original vision parity and vision serving remain explicitly unrun. The whole remaining tail did not fit its original15minute allowance; this independently bounded subset keeps the exact original commands, inputs, targets and checks with a600second outer ceiling. The earlier full run and its resource failures remain mandatory failed evidence; success here cannot replace them or claim a full verification pass. VM recorded by outer ownership guard; no skipped tail gate can pass.',
 'frozen_at':datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),'deadline_utc':sys.argv[1],
 'build':verified_build(B),'native_prerequisites':native,'native_contract_sha256':digest(NATIVE_PACKET/'qualification-contract.json'),'wrapper_sha256':digest(Path(__file__)),'policy':POLICY,
 'environment':{'SLOTSTREAM_TEST_BINARY':str(B),'SLOTSTREAM_VERIFY_OUT':str(OUT/'verification')},
 'command':['bash',str(P/'verify-remaining.sh')], 'selected_script_sha256':digest(P/'verify-remaining.sh'), 'verify_source_sha256':digest(ROOT/'Tools/verify.sh'), 'predecessor_manifest_sha256':digest(ROOT/'.build/optimization/current-candidate-gates-v307/full/manifest.json'),
 'drivers':{str(f.relative_to(ROOT)):digest(f) for f in sorted((ROOT/'Tools').rglob('*')) if f.is_file() and f.suffix in ['.sh','.py','.mjs','.json','.swift','.txt'] and '__pycache__' not in f.parts},
 'limits':'No build, app closure, default activation or paid service. Existing verified local model only. Source/binary/Metal pinned; files saved before exit.'}
with (P/'protocol.json').open('x') as protocol:protocol.write(json.dumps(S,indent=2)+'\n')
OUT.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False);started=time.monotonic();r={'passed':False,'protocol_sha256':digest(P/'protocol.json')}
def interrupted(number,_frame):raise KeyboardInterrupt(f'full qualification interrupted by signal{number}')
signal.signal(signal.SIGINT,interrupted);signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM,interrupted)
# Capture legacy fixed output files on both sides of this exact run. An
# unchanged/stale output is retained as history, never asserted to be new evidence.
legacy_names=sorted(set(re.findall(r'/tmp/(ssv[\w.-]+)',(ROOT/'Tools/verify.sh').read_text())))
def capture_legacy(phase):
 folder=P/('legacy-output-'+phase);folder.mkdir(exist_ok=False);rows=[]
 for name in legacy_names:
  path=Path('/tmp')/name
  if path.is_symlink():
   rows.append({'path':str(path),'kind':'symlink','target':os.readlink(path)});continue
  if not path.is_file():continue
  stat=path.stat();sha=digest(path);shutil.copy2(path,folder/name)
  rows.append({'path':str(path),'sha256':sha,'bytes':stat.st_size,'mtime_ns':stat.st_mtime_ns,
               'observed_after_run_start':stat.st_mtime_ns>=int(legacy_started_wall*1e9)})
 (folder/'inventory.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2)+'\n');return rows
legacy_started_wall=time.time()
r['legacy_output_before']=capture_legacy('before')
saved={k:v for k,v in os.environ.items() if k.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_','SS_DEBUG'))}
with (OUT/'pressure.samples.jsonl').open('x') as observations:
 def snapshot():
  state=vm_snapshot();pressure=pressure_snapshot();observations.write(json.dumps({'seconds':time.monotonic()-started,'vm':state,'pressure':pressure})+'\n');observations.flush();require_normal(pressure);return state
 try:
  r['before']=preflight(21);snapshot();assert not competing_jobs()
  assert (ROOT/'.venv31/bin/python').exists()
  for k in saved:os.environ.pop(k,None)
  os.environ.update(S['environment'])
  with (OUT/'stdout.txt').open('w') as stdout,(OUT/'stderr.txt').open('w') as stderr:
   result=guarded_run(S['command'],cwd=ROOT,stdout=stdout,stderr=stderr,record_path=OUT/'memory.json',snapshot=snapshot,policy=POLICY,classification=S['classification'])
  r['exit_code']=result.returncode
  report=(OUT/'stdout.txt').read_text();summary=re.findall(r'^passed (\d+), failed (\d+)$',report,re.M)
  r['summary']=summary;r['skips']=re.findall(r'^SKIP.*$',report,re.M);r['failures']=re.findall(r'^FAIL.*$',report,re.M)
  r['passed']=result.returncode==0 and len(summary)==1 and int(summary[0][0])>0 and summary[0][1]=='0' and not r['skips'] and not r['failures']
 except BaseException as e:r['error']=f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 finally:
  for k in list(os.environ):
   if k.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_','SS_DEBUG')):os.environ.pop(k,None)
  os.environ.update(saved)
  r['elapsed_seconds']=time.monotonic()-started
  # Preserve exact fixed outputs, including failed-check artifacts.
  try:r['legacy_output_after']=capture_legacy('after')
  except BaseException as error:r.setdefault('cleanup_errors',{})['legacy_output_capture']=str(error)
  # Primary result is durable before independent restoration checks.
  (OUT/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2)+'\n')
  for n,f in {'after':vm_snapshot,'pressure':pressure_snapshot,'candidate_unchanged':lambda:verified_build(B)==S['build'],
   'drivers_unchanged':lambda:all(digest(ROOT/n)==h for n,h in S['drivers'].items()) and digest(P/'verify-remaining.sh')==S['selected_script_sha256'],
   'remaining_jobs':competing_jobs,'model_lock_free':lambda:bool(preflight(0))}.items():
   try:r[n]=f()
   except BaseException as e:r.setdefault('cleanup_errors',{})[n]=f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
  r['within_reservation']=time.time()<=DEADLINE
  r['passed']=bool(r['passed'] and not r.get('cleanup_errors') and r.get('candidate_unchanged') and r.get('drivers_unchanged')
   and not r.get('remaining_jobs') and r.get('model_lock_free') and r.get('pressure',{}).get('level')==1 and r['within_reservation'])
  (OUT/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2)+'\n')
print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in r.items() if k not in ['before','after']}),flush=True)
raise SystemExit(0 if r['passed'] else 1)
````

## /tmp/slotstream-optimization-execution/verification-text-tail-v315/verify-remaining.sh

SHA-256 `2195209fd6160d96c06c6b65ea35d43ca43b6441f35c4f790368cbb17cab3571`.

````text
#!/bin/bash
set -eo pipefail
BIN=${SLOTSTREAM_TEST_BINARY:-${BIN:-.build/release/slotstream}}
VERIFY_OUT=${SLOTSTREAM_VERIFY_OUT:-.build/verification-$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)}
mkdir -p "$VERIFY_OUT"
export BIN SLOTSTREAM_TEST_BINARY="$BIN"
CHECK_INDEX=0
safety_before() {
  python3 - "$1" <<'PYSAFE'
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, 'Tools')
from prefill_bench import preflight
preflight(float(sys.argv[1]))
PYSAFE
}
run_model() { safety_before 13 || return 2; "$@"; }
# Keep the selected path out of evaluated snippets, including substitutions.
run_binary() { "$BIN" "$@"; }
PASS=0; FAIL=0
check() {
  CHECK_INDEX=$((CHECK_INDEX+1))
  local record="$VERIFY_OUT/check-$CHECK_INDEX.txt"
  printf '%s\n%s\n' "$1" "$2" > "$record"
  if [[ "$2" == "run_binary "* ]]; then safety_before 13 || return 2; fi
  if eval "$2" >>"$record" 2>&1; then echo "PASS  $1"; PASS=$((PASS+1))
  else echo "FAIL  $1 (details: $record)"; FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); fi
}
QPID=""
cleanup() {
  if [ -n "$QPID" ]; then
    kill "$QPID" 2>/dev/null || true
    wait "$QPID" 2>/dev/null || true
  fi
}
trap cleanup EXIT INT TERM


SMALL_MEMORY=8.1
BIG_MEMORY=10
ECBIG=960
echo "== serving robustness (inputs that used to crash or corrupt output) =="
echo "== behavioural sanity: has the conversion lost anything obvious? =="
# NOT the FP8 comparison the plan calls for (see N4) — that needs an inference
# credential for Qwen3.8-Flash-Next FP8, which is not provisioned. This catches
# gross quantization or architecture damage and gates future re-quantization.
# `set -e` is on, so every step here has to be failure-tolerant on purpose:
# a `kill` of an already-dead server, and a `wait` on a killed one (which
# returns 143), both abort the whole battery otherwise. That is exactly how an
# earlier version of this block silently truncated the run after this gate.
safety_before 13
"$BIN" serve --port 11467 --memory-gb $BIG_MEMORY >/tmp/ssv_q.log 2>&1 &
QPID=$!
for _ in $(seq 1 120); do
  if curl -s --max-time 3 http://127.0.0.1:11467/api/version >/dev/null 2>&1; then break; fi
  sleep 2
done
if Tools/quality_probe.sh 11467; then
  echo "PASS  behavioural quality probe (15 items)"; PASS=$((PASS+1))
else
  echo "FAIL  behavioural quality probe"; FAIL=$((FAIL+1))
fi
kill $QPID 2>/dev/null || true
wait $QPID 2>/dev/null || true
QPID=""

echo "== weights behind a symlink (Foundation will not list a symlinked dir) =="
MODEL_DIR=models/qwen38-flash-next-mlx-4bit
[ -d "$MODEL_DIR" ] || MODEL_DIR="$HOME/.slotstream/models/qwen38-flash-next-mlx-4bit"
SYM=/tmp/ssv_symlink_model
rm -f "$SYM"; ln -s "$(cd "$MODEL_DIR" && pwd)" "$SYM"
check "run through a symlinked model dir"  "run_binary run --model \"\$SYM\" --memory-gb $SMALL_MEMORY --max-tokens 1 --greedy --prompt hi"
rm -f "$SYM"

safety_before 13
if Tools/api_robustness.sh 11466 13; then
  echo "PASS  serving robustness suite"; PASS=$((PASS+1))
else
  echo "FAIL  serving robustness suite"; FAIL=$((FAIL+1))
fi


echo
echo "passed $PASS, failed $FAIL"
[ $FAIL -eq 0 ]

````

````text
from pathlib import Path
import datetime,json,os,re,shutil,signal,sys,time
ROOT=Path('/Users/carlos/Projects/slotstream');P=Path(__file__).parent
sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'Tools'))
from prefill_bench import digest,preflight,vm_snapshot
from serve_bench import competing_jobs,verified_build
from optimization_readiness import pressure_snapshot,require_normal
from optimization_serial_build import guarded_run
import optimization_campaign as campaign
B=ROOT/'.build/optimization/joined-state-eval-build-v304/candidate/slotstream'
OUT=ROOT/'.build/optimization/current-candidate-gates-v316/vision-tail'
# The reservation must be granted by the peer before invocation; the argument
# binds a concrete handback deadline and never authorizes extending it.
DEADLINE=datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(sys.argv[1].replace('Z','+00:00')).timestamp()
remaining=int(DEADLINE-time.time()-30)
if remaining<420:raise RuntimeError('original vision subset requires at least7minutes within the granted interval')
POLICY={'startup_reclaimable_bytes':21_000_000_000,'minimum_live_reclaimable_bytes':3_000_000_000,
 'maximum_owned_rss_bytes':17_500_000_000,'sample_interval_seconds':.2,
 'maximum_build_seconds':min(420,remaining),'stop_on_new_swapouts':False}
NATIVE_PACKET=ROOT/'.build/optimization/joined-state-eval-campaign-v305'
native=campaign.status(NATIVE_PACKET)['stages'][:3]
assert [r['stage'] for r in native]==['native/'+n for n in campaign.NATIVE] and all(r['status']=='passed' for r in native)
S={'classification':'Only the unchanged, previously unexecuted VISION subset of full verification: native tower dump, independent mlx0.31.1 float32 reference, and original known-content vision serving on every declared surface. The text subset passed separately. All original photographs, preprocessing/chunk/context/capacity settings, commands and assertions remain unchanged. The independently bounded subset has a420second outer ceiling; timeout or any skipped gate fails this subset. The earlier full run and its resource failures remain mandatory failed evidence; success here cannot replace them or claim a full verification pass. VM recorded by outer ownership guard; no skipped tail gate can pass.',
 'frozen_at':datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),'deadline_utc':sys.argv[1],
 'build':verified_build(B),'native_prerequisites':native,'native_contract_sha256':digest(NATIVE_PACKET/'qualification-contract.json'),'wrapper_sha256':digest(Path(__file__)),'policy':POLICY,
 'environment':{'SLOTSTREAM_TEST_BINARY':str(B),'SLOTSTREAM_VERIFY_OUT':str(OUT/'verification')},
 'command':['bash',str(P/'verify-remaining.sh')], 'selected_script_sha256':digest(P/'verify-remaining.sh'), 'verify_source_sha256':digest(ROOT/'Tools/verify.sh'), 'predecessor_manifest_sha256':digest(ROOT/'.build/optimization/current-candidate-gates-v307/full/manifest.json'),
 'drivers':{str(f.relative_to(ROOT)):digest(f) for f in sorted((ROOT/'Tools').rglob('*')) if f.is_file() and f.suffix in ['.sh','.py','.mjs','.json','.swift','.txt'] and '__pycache__' not in f.parts},
 'limits':'No build, app closure, default activation or paid service. Existing verified local model only. Source/binary/Metal pinned; files saved before exit.'}
with (P/'protocol.json').open('x') as protocol:protocol.write(json.dumps(S,indent=2)+'\n')
OUT.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False);started=time.monotonic();r={'passed':False,'protocol_sha256':digest(P/'protocol.json')}
def interrupted(number,_frame):raise KeyboardInterrupt(f'full qualification interrupted by signal{number}')
signal.signal(signal.SIGINT,interrupted);signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM,interrupted)
# Capture legacy fixed output files on both sides of this exact run. An
# unchanged/stale output is retained as history, never asserted to be new evidence.
legacy_names=sorted(set(re.findall(r'/tmp/(ssv[\w.-]+)',(ROOT/'Tools/verify.sh').read_text())))
def capture_legacy(phase):
 folder=P/('legacy-output-'+phase);folder.mkdir(exist_ok=False);rows=[]
 for name in legacy_names:
  path=Path('/tmp')/name
  if path.is_symlink():
   rows.append({'path':str(path),'kind':'symlink','target':os.readlink(path)});continue
  if not path.is_file():continue
  stat=path.stat();sha=digest(path);shutil.copy2(path,folder/name)
  rows.append({'path':str(path),'sha256':sha,'bytes':stat.st_size,'mtime_ns':stat.st_mtime_ns,
               'observed_after_run_start':stat.st_mtime_ns>=int(legacy_started_wall*1e9)})
 (folder/'inventory.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2)+'\n');return rows
legacy_started_wall=time.time()
r['legacy_output_before']=capture_legacy('before')
saved={k:v for k,v in os.environ.items() if k.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_','SS_DEBUG'))}
with (OUT/'pressure.samples.jsonl').open('x') as observations:
 def snapshot():
  state=vm_snapshot();pressure=pressure_snapshot();observations.write(json.dumps({'seconds':time.monotonic()-started,'vm':state,'pressure':pressure})+'\n');observations.flush();require_normal(pressure);return state
 try:
  r['before']=preflight(21);snapshot();assert not competing_jobs()
  assert (ROOT/'.venv31/bin/python').exists()
  for k in saved:os.environ.pop(k,None)
  os.environ.update(S['environment'])
  with (OUT/'stdout.txt').open('w') as stdout,(OUT/'stderr.txt').open('w') as stderr:
   result=guarded_run(S['command'],cwd=ROOT,stdout=stdout,stderr=stderr,record_path=OUT/'memory.json',snapshot=snapshot,policy=POLICY,classification=S['classification'])
  r['exit_code']=result.returncode
  report=(OUT/'stdout.txt').read_text();summary=re.findall(r'^passed (\d+), failed (\d+)$',report,re.M)
  r['summary']=summary;r['skips']=re.findall(r'^SKIP.*$',report,re.M);r['failures']=re.findall(r'^FAIL.*$',report,re.M)
  r['passed']=result.returncode==0 and len(summary)==1 and int(summary[0][0])>0 and summary[0][1]=='0' and not r['skips'] and not r['failures']
 except BaseException as e:r['error']=f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 finally:
  for k in list(os.environ):
   if k.startswith(('SLOTSTREAM_','SS_DEBUG')):os.environ.pop(k,None)
  os.environ.update(saved)
  r['elapsed_seconds']=time.monotonic()-started
  # Preserve exact fixed outputs, including failed-check artifacts.
  try:r['legacy_output_after']=capture_legacy('after')
  except BaseException as error:r.setdefault('cleanup_errors',{})['legacy_output_capture']=str(error)
  # Primary result is durable before independent restoration checks.
  (OUT/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2)+'\n')
  for n,f in {'after':vm_snapshot,'pressure':pressure_snapshot,'candidate_unchanged':lambda:verified_build(B)==S['build'],
   'drivers_unchanged':lambda:all(digest(ROOT/n)==h for n,h in S['drivers'].items()) and digest(P/'verify-remaining.sh')==S['selected_script_sha256'],
   'remaining_jobs':competing_jobs,'model_lock_free':lambda:bool(preflight(0))}.items():
   try:r[n]=f()
   except BaseException as e:r.setdefault('cleanup_errors',{})[n]=f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
  r['within_reservation']=time.time()<=DEADLINE
  r['passed']=bool(r['passed'] and not r.get('cleanup_errors') and r.get('candidate_unchanged') and r.get('drivers_unchanged')
   and not r.get('remaining_jobs') and r.get('model_lock_free') and r.get('pressure',{}).get('level')==1 and r['within_reservation'])
  (OUT/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2)+'\n')
print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in r.items() if k not in ['before','after']}),flush=True)
raise SystemExit(0 if r['passed'] else 1)

````

## /tmp/slotstream-optimization-execution/verification-vision-tail-v316/verify-remaining.sh

SHA-256 `49bcd478a5c508cb4acea5706a22bf1cda349a142d88b64ee9a45af742ef16f0`.

````text
#!/bin/bash
set -eo pipefail
BIN=${SLOTSTREAM_TEST_BINARY:-${BIN:-.build/release/slotstream}}
VERIFY_OUT=${SLOTSTREAM_VERIFY_OUT:-.build/verification-$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)}
mkdir -p "$VERIFY_OUT"
export BIN SLOTSTREAM_TEST_BINARY="$BIN"
CHECK_INDEX=0
safety_before() {
  python3 - "$1" <<'PYSAFE'
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, 'Tools')
from prefill_bench import preflight
preflight(float(sys.argv[1]))
PYSAFE
}
run_model() { safety_before 13 || return 2; "$@"; }
# Keep the selected path out of evaluated snippets, including substitutions.
run_binary() { "$BIN" "$@"; }
PASS=0; FAIL=0
check() {
  CHECK_INDEX=$((CHECK_INDEX+1))
  local record="$VERIFY_OUT/check-$CHECK_INDEX.txt"
  printf '%s\n%s\n' "$1" "$2" > "$record"
  if [[ "$2" == "run_binary "* ]]; then safety_before 13 || return 2; fi
  if eval "$2" >>"$record" 2>&1; then echo "PASS  $1"; PASS=$((PASS+1))
  else echo "FAIL  $1 (details: $record)"; FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); fi
}
QPID=""
cleanup() {
  if [ -n "$QPID" ]; then
    kill "$QPID" 2>/dev/null || true
    wait "$QPID" 2>/dev/null || true
  fi
}
trap cleanup EXIT INT TERM


SMALL_MEMORY=8.1
BIG_MEMORY=10
ECBIG=960
echo "== vision =="
# The tower against an independent implementation. It loads 0.9 GB of vision
# tensors and none of the 105 GB trunk, so it is cheap and can run anywhere the
# weights are. mlx 0.31.1 for the same reason the parity goldens use it.
VP="$VERIFY_OUT/vision-parity"
if [ -x .venv31/bin/python ]; then
  check "vision tower dumps its pixels and embeddings" \
    'run_binary vision-parity --out "$VP"'
  safety_before 7
  if .venv31/bin/python Tools/vision_ref.py "$VP" | tail -8; then
    echo "PASS  vision tower matches the float32 reference within the bf16 band"
    PASS=$((PASS+1))
  else
    echo "FAIL  vision tower parity"; FAIL=$((FAIL+1))
  fi
else
  echo "SKIP  vision parity (no .venv31; see CLAUDE.md for the mlx 0.31.1 venv)"
  FAIL=$((FAIL+1)) # Required full vision acceptance did not run.
fi

# Every serving surface, with a real picture, against a real server. The model
# has to name what is in the photograph: a tower wired to the wrong positions
# still answers fluently, and nothing cheaper than this notices.
#
# Full original photographs require a 3.99 GB attention workspace reservation.
# Keep this explicit profile local to this server: ordinary equality/quality
# gates still use BIG_MEMORY. The 10 GB predecessor now correctly refuses the
# larger image before dispatch, and that counterexample remains in db/.
VISION_MEMORY=14.5
VISION_PREFILL=3072
NEED_GB=$(awk "BEGIN{print $VISION_MEMORY + 6}")
AVAIL_GB=$("$BIN" doctor --json 2>/dev/null | python3 -c 'import json,sys; print(json.load(sys.stdin).get("device_available_gb", 0))' 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
if [ "$(awk "BEGIN{print ($AVAIL_GB < $NEED_GB)}")" = "1" ]; then
  echo "SKIP  vision serving suite (only ${AVAIL_GB} GB reclaimable, needs ${NEED_GB})"
  FAIL=$((FAIL+1)) # Required full vision acceptance did not run.
  echo "      re-run after preflight: SLOTSTREAM_PREFILL_CHUNK=$VISION_PREFILL SLOTSTREAM_BENCH_DETAILS=1 $BIN serve --memory-gb $VISION_MEMORY --mtp off --vision on --max-context 32768 --max-prefill-wait 0 --no-elastic --port 11468"
  echo "      then: python3 Tools/vision_serving.py 11468"
else
safety_before "$NEED_GB"
SLOTSTREAM_PREFILL_CHUNK="$VISION_PREFILL" SLOTSTREAM_BENCH_DETAILS=1 "$BIN" serve --memory-gb "$VISION_MEMORY" --mtp off --vision on --max-context 32768 --max-prefill-wait 0 --no-elastic --port 11468 > /tmp/ssv-vision-serve.log 2>&1 &
QPID=$!
for _ in $(seq 1 120); do
  if grep -q "listening on" /tmp/ssv-vision-serve.log 2>/dev/null; then break; fi
  sleep 1
done
if python3 Tools/vision_serving.py 11468; then
  echo "PASS  vision serving suite"; PASS=$((PASS+1))
else
  echo "FAIL  vision serving suite"; FAIL=$((FAIL+1))
fi
kill "$QPID" 2>/dev/null || true
wait "$QPID" 2>/dev/null || true
QPID=""
fi

echo
echo "passed $PASS, failed $FAIL"
[ $FAIL -eq 0 ]

````
